Owned by `build-planner`. One objective per cycle. Specialists read their dispatch brief, not this file.

## RUN DIRECTIVE (owner, cycle 20): CONTINUOUS TO COMPLETION
Do not stop between tasks. Keep looping — build endpoint → QA → re-wire client → next port — until the ENTIRE M3.2 port list is done and M4 cutover is reached. Stop ONLY for: a genuine owner money/product decision with no other unblocked work; a task that failed QA twice; a safety-rail action (commit/push, destructive SQL, file deletion — all need sign-off); or M4 (delete Firebase) which needs explicit sign-off. Report a STATUS block per cycle and keep going.

## GROUP I (owner-directed, cycle 98) — DASHBOARDS, NAVIGATION & ANALYTICS USABILITY
New owner-directed deliverable group promoted alongside G/H (A–F checklist stays fully `[x]`; this is enrichment, not a reopened box). Owner directive (full autonomy, functional focus). Decomposed into five items:
- **[x] I1 — Broken-button audit + fix (dashboards + nav).** Every button on `user_dashboard`, `admin_dashboard`, `analytics`, `user_analytics` and the shared sidebar/topbar shell must either do something or be removed. Named dead ones: `notificationsBtn` (bell) does nothing; an admin on the USER view cannot switch to the ADMIN view (the "Admin View" footer / portal-toggle is broken); the card info "i" helper does nothing. — DONE (cycle 98): audit found every reported control already WIRED by cycles 94-96; no fix needed.
- **[x] I2 — Month filter + per-month summaries** on BOTH dashboards (current-month default + pick-a-month), month-scoped summary figures. Re-aggregate already-fetched data (`payments.obligations`/`statement`/`payments.list?year`) where possible; a month-scoped server aggregation is a backend slice first only if genuinely needed. — DONE, QA PASS (cycle 99).
- **[x] I3 — Analytics "Financial Trends" build-out** on `analytics.html`: add filtering + real accounting figures by reusing `payments.accountingSummary` (H5) + existing trend data. — DONE, QA PASS (cycle 100): new `#accountingFiguresBlock` renders H5's ten server-computed money-string figures, display-only, zero client math.
- **[x] I4 — `user_analytics.html` usability build-out**: currently bare and nothing is clickable — make it interactive with real content/actions. — DONE, QA PASS (all 4 slices, cycle 108). A (5 loan stat-cards → click-through nav to `loan_payments.html`, HTML-only) DONE+QA cycle 105; B (new member-gated `payments.groupStats` endpoint — memberCount / groupTotalContributed / activeLoanCount only, explicitly excludes admin-only figures like cashPosition — + 3 previously-dead Group Stats cards wired) DONE+QA cycle 107; C ("show all" toggle on Recent Activity capped at 10 + expandable Contribution Trend capped at last-6, pure client re-render on already-fetched data, no new API calls) DONE+QA cycle 107; D (contribution/arrears breakdown popover on 4 cards — additive server-computed `contributionBreakdown` seed/monthly/service-fee subtotals on `payments.obligations` that provably reconcile with the existing total, + a ported `.stat-card-popover` mechanism; one real CSS overlap bug found + fixed + re-verified) DONE+QA cycle 108. **I4 done → ALL of GROUP I (I1–I5) COMPLETE.**
- **[x] I5 — Card info "i" tooltip rollout**: make the helper work and add it to many cards across the dashboards/analytics. — DONE, QA PASS (both slices, cycle 104 closure). Slice 1 = analytics.html (18 cards, page-local `attachCardPopover()` + ported popover CSS); slice 2 = user_dashboard.html (3 hero-stat tiles, reusing this page's own cycle-94 `.hero-stat-popover` + `initHeroStatPopover`, no CSS port). See CYCLE 104.

**Sequencing (justify):** I1 first — concrete, high-visibility, low-risk bugs users hit immediately; and its fix also produces the working "i"-tooltip mechanism that I5 rolls out and the view-switch that admins need daily. Then I2 (month filter, pure re-aggregation, both dashboards), I3 (Financial Trends, reuses H5), I5 (tooltip rollout on top of I1's mechanism), I4 (user_analytics build-out, largest). Money items stay server-side; none of I adds client money math.

### GROUP I — CLOSED (cycle 108). All five items `[x]`. What members and admins can now DO that they could not before:
- **Both dashboards are now time-scoped.** A member on `user_dashboard` and an admin on `admin_dashboard` land on the current month by default and can pick any past month; the summary figures (a member's Contributed + Pending; an admin's Collections) re-scope instantly to the chosen month — pure client re-aggregation of already-loaded data, no new server calls, all maths still server-side in minor units. (I2)
- **The analytics page shows real accounting figures, not just charts.** The admin Financial Trends view now renders ten server-computed money figures (total contributed, disbursed, outstanding principal, interest earned, repayments received, penalties charged/collected/waived/outstanding, cash position) straight from the group accounting summary — display-only, no client arithmetic. (I3)
- **Every stat card can explain itself.** An "i" helper on 18 analytics cards and the user-dashboard hero tiles opens a plain-language popover describing what the number means — keyboard- and tap-accessible, reusing one proven mechanism. (I1 confirmed the mechanism was already wired; I5 rolled it out broadly.)
- **`user_analytics.html` went from a dead, read-only page to a fully interactive one.** A member can now: click any of 5 loan stat-cards to jump straight to their loan/repayment detail; see live group-transparency stats (member count, total group contributions, active loan count) via a new member-safe endpoint that deliberately withholds admin-only figures; expand Recent Activity ("show all" beyond the first 10) and the Contribution Trend (beyond the last 6 months) without a page reload; and open a breakdown popover on the contribution and arrears cards that splits Contributed into Seed / Monthly / Service-fee and Arrears into Contribution-arrears / Penalties-accrued — every subtotal server-computed and provably reconciling with the card total (no client money math). (I4)
- **No dead controls remain on the dashboards or nav shell** — the notification bell, admin↔user view switch, and card "i" helpers were all confirmed live. (I1)

**GROUP I is owner-directed enrichment layered on the already-complete A–F checklist — no PROJECT COMPLETE WHEN box was reopened.** With I closed and no owner directive pending, the project returns to its terminal PROJECT COMPLETE state (see the cycle-108 verdict below).

## CYCLE 99 RESOLUTION — I2 FRONTEND DISPATCH (2026-07-23) — **scout returned; client re-aggregation confirmed, no backend slice needed**
The cycle-99 scout appended "## Dashboard data→summary pipeline (cycle 99 scout)" to SYSTEM_MAP (lines 943-1047). Verdict from the map (not re-derived): **both dashboards already hold the raw per-record arrays** (`window.__dashboardData.payments` on user, `groupData.payments` on admin), each row carrying `month` (ENUM January…December) + `year` (INT) + `approvalStatus` — so month scoping is **pure client re-aggregation via each file's own integer-minor helpers; NO `?month` server call, NO schema, NO backend slice.** The Payment Calendar (user) and Collection Trends range-select (admin) already have their OWN period controls and are OUT of scope — this cycle adds a picker only to the **main summary stat band** on each page.

**Design decisions recorded (ambiguous ≠ blocked — reasonable village-banking flow, decided and proceeding):**
1. **Which figures re-scope:** only the figures whose source rows carry a contribution `month`/`year` — i.e. the monthly cash-flow money figures. Inherently cross-month / forward-looking figures are LEFT at their current values because forcing them into one month is misleading: **user** → re-scope **Contributed** + **Pending**; leave **Arrears** (cumulative obligation), **Loans** (loan state, not a contribution month), **Group Members**, **Next Payment** (forward-looking) unchanged. **admin** → re-scope **Collections**; leave **Active Loans**, **Pending** (mixed payments+loans operational queue), **Arrears** (group-wide server total, no per-record dates per scout §4) unchanged.
2. **Default = current calendar month** (owner directive: "current-month default"). Options = each month of the **current year** plus a leading **"Whole year (all months)"** value so the annual/lifetime view is never lost. Scope stays within the current year (matches the cycle-99 recorded "past months of the current cycle/year" assumption); a full year-picker is deferred to FUTURE IDEAS.
3. **Honesty on the semantic shift:** because the default flips these tiles from lifetime→month, the re-scoped tile's **label** must show the active scope (e.g. "Contributed · Jul" for a month, "Contributed · 2026" for whole-year) via `textContent` — never ambiguous. The "Whole year" option restores the annual figure.
4. **Re-aggregation is NOT new money math** — it re-sums already-settled amounts in **integer minor units** using each file's existing `toMinor`/`fromMinor`, mirroring the pattern the current pending-sum (user JS:483) and Collection-Trends sum (admin JS:319-484) already use. No floats, no new arithmetic rule.

Two dispatch briefs below operate on **disjoint file sets** (`user_dashboard.*` vs `admin_dashboard.*`) → **may run in PARALLEL (2 concurrent, satisfies the cap).**

## CYCLE 100 BUILD (2026-07-23) — I3: ADD GROUP-ACCOUNTING FIGURES BLOCK TO analytics.html — **BRIEF DISPATCHED**
Scout returned (SYSTEM_MAP "Analytics Financial Trends pipeline (cycle 100 scout)"). Confirmed: `payments.accountingSummary` (H5) is **not** called anywhere in `analytics_sql.js` (zero refs). This I3 slice adds that call + renders its ten server-computed money-string fields into a **new, separate** `#accountingFiguresBlock`, purely ADDITIVE. Traces to GROUP I / I3 (real accounting figures on analytics.html, reusing shipped H5).

**Design decisions recorded (ambiguous ≠ blocked):**
- **Layout:** the ten figures render as labeled cards via the file's existing `statCard(label, value)` helper (analytics_sql.js:424–434 — returns a `.breakdown-card` node, `textContent`-safe) appended into a new `<div id="accountingFiguresBlock" class="charts-grid">`. Reuses the exact CSS (`.charts-grid` / `.breakdown-card`) already used by the pie-stat cards, so **no CSS/HTML class guessing**. A section title ("Group Accounting Position") is prepended into the block as an `el("h3")` node (the `el("h3")` pattern is already used at analytics_sql.js:353) so no new HTML heading class is invented.
- **Labels (field → card title):** totalContributed → "Total Contributed"; totalDisbursed → "Total Disbursed"; outstandingLoanPrincipal → "Outstanding Loan Principal"; interestEarned → "Interest Earned"; loanRepaymentsReceived → "Loan Repayments Received"; penaltiesCharged → "Penalties Charged"; penaltiesCollected → "Penalties Collected"; penaltiesWaived → "Penalties Waived"; penaltiesOutstanding → "Penalties Outstanding"; cashPosition → "Cash Position". All ten shown (each is a distinct, already-labelled H5 figure).
- **Not month-filtered:** `payments.accountingSummary` is a single whole-group cumulative server total (like cashPosition), NOT a per-record array, so it is NOT re-scoped by the Week/Month/Year tabs (those only rescope the trend chart). The accounting block shows the cumulative group position independent of the tab — this is exactly why the scout advised keeping it separate from the participation-triggered `#chartContainer` cards. Recorded as the defensible reading; no filter control added to this block.
- **Display helper:** `formatCurrency` (imported analytics_sql.js:56 from `./utils_financial.js`) accepts a bare major-unit money string like `"1000.00"` (parses via `Number`, outputs `"MWK 1,000.00"`) — H5 fields come from `money_from_minor` (major-unit strings), so `formatCurrency(summary.field)` is correct and introduces ZERO arithmetic. (Do NOT use `formatCurrencyFromMinor` — these are not minor units.)
- **Failure isolation:** the new fetch is wrapped in its own try/catch so an `accountingSummary` error (e.g. DB throttle) surfaces a toast + empty state in the block but does NOT abort the rest of `loadAnalytics()`.
- **Role gating:** analytics.html is already an admin surface (the group selector is populated from `adminGroups` only, analytics_sql.js:178) and the H5 endpoint re-checks `require_role` (PAYMENT_ADMIN_ROLES) server-side — no client gate added. Assumption noted: the page's existing admin scoping stands.

### REUSE-FIRST OUTCOME (targeted reads this cycle — NO scout needed)
Read `my_obligations` (payments.php:903–1064), its summary helper `payment_obligations_summary_minor` (payments.php:775–798), and `user_analytics_sql.js`'s obligations handling (68/213/225–268). Findings:
- **The `payments.obligations` response is ALREADY fetched and stored client-side** in the module var `obligations` (user_analytics_sql.js:68, assigned at 213). `renderTopStats()` (225) already reads `summary.contributed`→`totalContributed` and `summary.arrears`→`totalArrears` and setTexts all 4 cards (250/253/255/258). So the card TOTALS are already client-side.
- **Arrears-vs-penalty split = ZERO new work, already client-side:** the response's `summary` block (payments.php:1058–1062) carries `arrears` AND `penaltyAccrued` as two DISTINCT server-computed totals. The arrears popover just renders both — no summing, no server change.
- **Contribution seed/monthly/service-fee split = the ONE genuine gap.** The response gives `seedMoney.amountPaid` and `serviceFee.amountPaid` as single figures (present client-side), but the MONTHLY paid subtotal is spread across 12 `monthlyContributions.months[]` rows and is NOT pre-summed. Summing those 12 money strings client-side would be **client-side money math on money strings — forbidden** by the money rule ("keep maths server-side in minor units"). The server ALREADY computes exactly this sum in `payment_obligations_summary_minor()` (payments.php:781–785, the `$monthlyContributedMinor` loop is right there implicitly in `$contributedMinor`) in integer minor units — it just doesn't emit the per-component subtotals. So the money-safe path is: extend that ONE server helper to also return `seedContributedMinor` / `monthlyContributedMinor` / `feeContributedMinor`, and have `my_obligations()` emit a `contributionBreakdown` object. Same-assembler guarantee: the three subtotals sum to the existing `summary.contributed` and cannot drift (identical source figures, same helper) — mirrors the CSV/JSON shared-assembler lesson.
- **Conclusion: Slice D is BACKEND-FIRST (small, additive, money-critical) then FRONTEND (popover port).** Contribution breakdown needs the tiny server subtotal emission; arrears breakdown is pure frontend on already-present fields.

### MECHANISM DECISION — port analytics.html's `.stat-card-popover` system (lowest-risk, proven, reuse-first)
This page has NO popover/modal CSS. Rather than invent one or build a heavier modal (no modal exists on this page to copy cheaply; a modal needs backdrop + open/close state), **port the self-contained, already-QA'd popover mechanism from analytics.html** (built for I5 slice-1). Why it fits best:
- Its CSS (`analytics.html` inline `<style>` 236–320) ALREADY targets `.page-stat` (this page's top-row card type) for `position: relative` + hover/focus-within reveal + the `.page-stats { overflow: visible }` clip-fix + the `.stat-card-info-toggle` "i" button + the `.stat-card-popover` box. Only adaptation: add `.stat-card` (this page's Your-Overview card type) alongside `.page-stat`/`.breakdown-card` in the `position:relative` group and the reveal selectors (this page's `.stat-card` already has `position: relative` at user_analytics.html:31, so that part is a no-op but harmless to include).
- Its JS (`analytics_sql.js` 596–677: `attachCardPopover` / `closeAllCardPopovers` / the module-scope outside-click listener) is `createElement`/`textContent` only (no innerHTML), keyboard-accessible (aria-expanded, Enter/Space/Escape), tap-toggles via `.open` — matches every project rail. It is ported (not imported — analytics_sql.js stays untouched), adapted to build STRUCTURED breakdown rows instead of a single `infoText` string.
- Popover rows are built as plain stacked `<div>`s with `textContent = "<label>: " + formatCurrency(...)` — NO new row CSS needed (block divs stack inside the ported popover's padding), NO innerHTML, NO client money math (every value is a server money-string rendered via the existing `formatCurrency(numberOf(...))`).
- **ASSUMPTION recorded (UX, sensibly decided — not owner's call):** the contributed popover always shows all three rows (Seed money / Monthly contributions / Service fee) even when a component is 0 or service fee is unconfigured (feeContributedMinor=0 → "MWK 0.00"), for a consistent, predictable breakdown; the arrears popover shows two rows (Contribution arrears / Penalties accrued). All 4 cards get a popover (the two contributed cards share the contributed content; the two arrears cards share the arrears content) since the recorded scope names all four ids.

## CYCLE 106 (2026-07-24) — group I, I4 SLICE B: **REUSE-FIRST CHECK DONE → BACKEND BRIEF DISPATCHED (member group-stats endpoint)**
Traces to GROUP I / I4, Slice B (the member-callable group-stats endpoint + the 3 dead Group Stats cards `#groupTotalMembers`/`#groupTotalCollections`/`#groupActiveLoans`). Slice A (loan-card click-through) is COMPLETE + QA-PASSED. The Slice-B in-scope + guardrail decision was recorded in cycle 105 (member transparency aggregates, read-only, no money rule, no owner confirmation) — NOT re-litigated here.

### REUSE-FIRST OUTCOME (targeted reads, no scout needed)
No drop-in member-callable group-aggregate endpoint exists to call as-is, BUT every piece of SQL/logic the new handler needs is already present in `api/handlers/payments.php` and `api/handlers/loans.php` and only needs to be assembled into one member-gated function — so an EXACT-LINE backend brief is writable directly (no scout). Confirmed by reading:
- **Roles constant (mirror verbatim):** `payments.php:52` `const PAYMENT_ANY_MEMBER_ROLES = ['member', 'admin', 'senior_admin', 'treasurer'];` — this IS the member-gate the guardrail requires. (`PAYMENT_ADMIN_ROLES` at line 51 is the admin-only set — do NOT use it here.)
- **Settled-contribution total (mirror the H5 pattern):** `group_accounting_summary()` (`payments.php:1204–1298`) computes `totalContributedMinor` at lines 1215–1224 as `SELECT amountPaid FROM payments WHERE groupId=:groupId AND approvalStatus IN ('approved','completed')`, summed **ROW-WISE via `money_to_minor(trim((string)$row['amountPaid']))`** (never SQL `SUM()`), then emitted via `money_from_minor(...)`. This is the exact definition to reuse for `groupTotalContributed`. But that whole handler is `require_role($groupId, PAYMENT_ADMIN_ROLES)` (line 1211) and also returns cashPosition/penaltiesWaived/etc. — the admin-only figures the guardrail forbids — so it CANNOT be member-scoped wholesale; only its contribution-loop is reused.
- **Active-member query (reuse verbatim):** `group_arrears_summary()` (`payments.php:1085`) at lines 1105–1111 uses `SELECT uid FROM members WHERE groupId=:groupId AND status = 'active'` — the codebase's established active-member convention (comment: "Active members only — a suspended/inactive member's obligations are not the group's live receivable"). Reuse `status = 'active'`; do NOT invent a status field.
- **Active-loan status set (reuse the named constant):** `loans.php:31` `const LOAN_ACTIVE_STATUSES = ['pending', 'approved', 'disbursed'];` (comment line 30: "Statuses that count against a member's active-loan allowance"). This is the codebase's canonical "active loan" set and best matches a card literally labelled *Active Loans*. (The alternative `DISBURSED_LOAN_STATUSES = ['approved','disbursed','completed','defaulted']` — `financial_reports_sql.js:63`, also used by group_accounting_summary — is an "ever-issued" set that counts COMPLETED/DEFAULTED loans as active, wrong for a live "active" count.) **ASSUMPTION recorded:** activeLoanCount = loans in the group whose `status` ∈ `LOAN_ACTIVE_STATUSES` (`pending`,`approved`,`disbursed`) — this counts a pending request as "in progress," matching the constant's own semantics; the excluded alternative (completed/defaulted) is intentionally not counted because those are no longer live. Defensible and uses an existing named set — not invented.

## CYCLE 105 (2026-07-24) — group I, I4 (user_analytics.html usability build-out): **SLICED + SLICE A FRONTEND BRIEF DISPATCHED**
Traces to GROUP I / I4 ("currently bare and nothing is clickable — make it interactive with real content/actions"), the LAST remaining GROUP I item. The cycle-104 scout mapped the full data/content pipeline (SYSTEM_MAP "user_analytics.html data & content pipeline (cycle 104 scout)"). I4 is the largest I-item and mixes pure-frontend reuse with one genuinely-new backend endpoint, so — mirroring I5's successful phased approach — it is decomposed into 3 slices and built one slice per cycle, safest-first.

### GROUP-STATS BACKEND DECISION (step 1 — recorded, NOT blocked)
The scout flagged the one non-frontend gap: the 3 Group Stats cards (`#groupTotalMembers`, `#groupTotalCollections`, `#groupActiveLoans`) are declared in the HTML (lines 272/276/280) but **never populated** because no member-callable group-wide-stats endpoint exists. **Decision: this is an ordinary, additive, member-facing transparency feature — NOT a critical change needing an owner-confirming question. It is IN SCOPE (Slice B).** Reasoning: in real village-banking, every member contributes to a shared pot and legitimately expects to see the group's overall health — how many members, how much the group has collected, how many loans are out — not just their own numbers. The figures are READ-ONLY aggregates (member count, total contributions collected, active-loan count), touch NO money rule and move NO money, and the UI intent was always present (the cards are pre-built, only the endpoint is missing). Precedent: financial_reports.html/analytics.html already expose whole-group figures to ADMINS via `payments.accountingSummary` (H5); a member-scoped, appropriately-limited READ-ONLY subset is a smaller, additive step, not a new capability class. **Guardrail recorded for Slice B:** expose ONLY non-sensitive aggregates (member count, group total contributed/collected, active-loan count) — explicitly NOT admin-only figures like cashPosition, penaltiesWaived, or any per-other-member breakdown. The endpoint is member-gated via `require_role($groupId, ['member','admin','senior_admin','treasurer'])` and scoped to the caller's own group. Because it is additive, read-only, and changes no money rule, no owner confirmation is required — it proceeds as a normal backend-first slice next.

### SLICE PLAN (safest-first, one per cycle — I5 precedent)
- **Slice A (THIS cycle — dispatched): loan-card click-through navigation.** Purely additive, HTML-only, **zero new API, zero JS-module change, zero CSS port, no money math.** Make the 5 loan-related stat cards navigate to the existing `loan_payments.html` on click / Enter / Space, mirroring user_dashboard.html's shipped-and-QA-passed `#activeLoansStat` inline-onclick pattern (user_dashboard.html:2196). This is the single safest, most self-contained, exact-line-known slice, so it goes first.
- **Slice B (NEXT — backend-first): member group-stats endpoint + wire the 3 dead Group Stats cards.** Per the decision above. Sequence: new read-only member-gated handler returning `{memberCount, groupTotalContributed(minor→string), activeLoanCount}` for the caller's group → `user_analytics_sql.js` calls it on group-select and `setText`s `#groupTotalMembers`/`#groupTotalCollections`/`#groupActiveLoans` → (no export needed). Requires a scout ONLY if no existing member-callable group-aggregate query can be reused; check `payments.obligations`/`groups.mine`/`loans.list` server side first (reuse-first — an admin cross-member aggregate may already exist to member-scope). Full brief written next cycle, not now.
- **Slice C (LATER, smaller UX gaps): "show all" recent activity + expandable contribution chart.** Both are client-side caps on ALREADY-fetched data — Recent Activity capped at 10 (`user_analytics_sql.js` renderRepaymentHistory, cap at line 336), chart capped at last-6 (renderContributionTrendChart, `.slice(-6)` at line 267). Add a "Show all" toggle for each. Cleanly scoped and exact-line per the scout map; in scope, deferred behind A and B. Full brief later.

**Deferred to a later slice (mechanism-based split, recorded so it isn't silently dropped):** the CONTRIBUTION/ARREARS cards (`#totalContributed`, `#totalArrears`, `#userTotalContributed`, `#userTotalArrears`, `#groupTotalContributed`) are NOT in Slice A. The scout's verdict for them is popover/modal (seed/monthly/service-fee or arrears-vs-penalty breakdown), which needs either an info-popover CSS port (this page has NONE — unlike user_dashboard) or component-split data the scout was unsure is cleanly available. That is a different mechanism and a larger diff than click-through, so it is a candidate for a later slice (likely folded into Slice C or a Slice D), NOT bundled into the safest first slice.

**Correctly OUT OF SCOPE (not FUTURE IDEAS — deliberately excluded per the scout):** the unshown loan-detail fields (interest rates, collateral, guarantor) and loan-payment principal/interest/penalty breakdown the scout found in the responses are admin-only detail, not member-visible — they stay unshown by design; do not surface them on this member page.

### I4 SLICE A — WHY IT'S THE RIGHT FIRST SLICE
The page today has **zero clickable cards** (scout: "all cards are display-only divs with no onclick handlers"). Click-through nav is the lowest-risk, highest-certainty win: it reuses a pattern that already shipped and QA-passed on user_dashboard, needs no CSS port (this page lacks popover CSS), no new helper, no JS-module edit, and no new/changed API call or figure. The 5 loan cards all have a natural, already-existing destination (`loan_payments.html`, which user_dashboard already navigates to param-free). Contribution/arrears cards have no equivalent member detail page to navigate to (user_dashboard opens a MODAL for those), so they are correctly excluded here and handled by a later, popover/modal slice.

### I5 SLICE 2 — user_dashboard.html: **DONE, QA PASS** → I5 FULLY COMPLETE (both pages)
The cycle-103 slice-2 brief landed and QA-PASSED. **I5 is now fully done: analytics.html (slice 1, cycle 103) + user_dashboard.html (slice 2, this cycle).** What now works on **user_dashboard.html**: the 3 hero-stat tiles that previously lacked an info affordance now have one, reusing this page's OWN cycle-94 `.hero-stat-popover` mechanism (NO admin CSS port):
- **#activeLoansStat** (a `<div role="button">`): got a unique `#activeLoansPopover` + `aria-describedby`, and `initHeroStatPopover("activeLoansStat","activeLoansPopover",{ignoreSelector:"#activeLoansBadge"})` wired in after the 3 already-wired tiles — so pointer click still navigates (existing inline `onclick`), and a keyboard/touch toggle is a pure gain; the badge is excluded from the toggle via `ignoreSelector`.
- **#membersStat** (an `<a href>`) and **#totalContributedStat** (a `<button onclick>`): got their content-filled `.hero-stat-popover` div + unique id + `aria-describedby` + `position: relative`, and rely on the page's existing `:hover`/`:focus-within` CSS reveal ONLY. They were deliberately NOT wired to `initHeroStatPopover` because its keydown handler unconditionally `preventDefault()`s Enter/Space (and `ignoreSelector` guards only the click handler, not keydown), which would hijack these tiles' native `<a>`/`<button>` keyboard activation. Their tap/Enter keeps doing the primary action (navigate / open modal) with zero regression.
- All 3 popovers have unique ids, non-empty hardcoded literal microcopy (zero injection risk — never interpolated from API/user data), and correct `aria-describedby`. This slice changed NO figure, no API call, no money math.
- **ui-designer spot-check** confirmed the reused `.hero-stat-popover` CSS renders correctly on all 3 new instances (including the two native `<a>`/`<button>` tiles that newly gained `position: relative`) — no CSS changes needed.

**ACCEPTED, INTENTIONAL LIMITATION recorded (not a bug, not actioned):** #membersStat/#totalContributedStat's popovers are reachable via hover + keyboard-focus but NOT via a touch tap (since they're intentionally not JS-toggle-wired). This is the correct tradeoff: the alternative — wiring the JS toggle — would hijack their native tap-to-navigate / tap-to-open-modal action, and a full touch-toggle would require restructuring them from `<a>`/`<button>` into a `role=button` div with an inner action element (a markup change out of scope for a tooltip rollout).

**QA-PROCESS LEARNING recorded (for future cycles on files with accumulated uncommitted diffs):** the FIRST review pass on this diff returned a FALSE FAIL — it mistook the pre-existing, already-shipped, already-QA-passed I2 month-filter feature (`applyDashboardMonthFilter`, `#dashboardMonthFilter`) for "undisclosed scope creep bundled into this cycle." Root cause: **this project never commits between build cycles (only the human commits), so `git diff` on a file accumulates ALL uncommitted work across multiple cycles, not just the newest slice.** A rescoped re-review with explicit context (pointing at the exact NEW ids/call-site to isolate — `#activeLoansPopover`/`#membersPopover`/`#totalContributedPopover` + the single new `initHeroStatPopover` call) correctly PASSED. **Rule for future QA dispatches on multi-cycle-accumulated files:** tell the auditor explicitly "feature X was already reviewed + passed in cycle N — do not re-flag it," or point it at the specific new identifiers to grep for, rather than a bare "review the diff." (Recorded here as a standing QA-dispatch rule for this project.)

What now works on **analytics.html**: every one of the **18 summary cards** now carries a working card-info "i" affordance — the 4 static `.page-stat` tiles (#totalIncome/#totalExpenses/#netProfit/#loanInterest), the 4 pie/breakdown `.breakdown-card` cards, and the 10 I3 accounting-figure cards — each with an "i" toggle button opening an explanatory plain-text popover (desktop hover/focus-within via CSS, click-toggle for touch, keyboard Enter/Space toggle + Escape close, outside-click closes; `aria-expanded`/`aria-controls`/`role=status`).
- **How it was built (recorded):** the popover CSS (`.stat-card-info-toggle` / `.stat-card-popover` / `.open`) was **ported into analytics.html's own inline `<style>`** (adapted from admin_dashboard.html; reveal rule retargeted to `.page-stat`/`.breakdown-card`, `position: relative` added to both). A **page-local `attachCardPopover()`** helper builds the toggle+popover (unique id via a module-scope counter, `createElement`+`textContent` only). `statCard()` gained an **optional, backward-compatible 3rd `infoText` arg** — every existing 2-arg caller still works. 18 pieces of **hardcoded plain-text microcopy** (never interpolated from API/user data → zero injection risk).
- **ui-designer polish found + fixed two real bugs:** (1) a z-index stacking issue where `.breakdown-card`'s pre-existing hover-transform could render a popover under a later sibling card; (2) text/toggle-button collisions in the dense 10-card accounting grid and the compact static tiles. Also verified `.page-stats { overflow: visible }` doesn't reintroduce a mobile horizontal-scroll risk (checked against pages.css breakpoints). CSS-only, no behaviour change.
- **QA verified (diff-only):** optional/backward-compatible param, unique popover ids surviving re-renders, correct `stopPropagation` + outside-click detection, real keyboard handlers, init wiring matches the actual DOM (`.page-stat` via `closest()`), **zero value-producing lines changed**, zero injection (all microcopy hardcoded literals), and admin_dashboard files completely untouched.
- **NON-BLOCKING pre-existing-debt note (out of scope, do NOT action here):** analytics_sql.js:304/315 compute `totalIncome`/`netProfit` via client-side addition of already-formatted figures rather than a server aggregate — pre-existing, untouched by this diff, logged for future awareness only.

### I5 SLICE 2 — user_dashboard.html (this cycle's dispatch)
Slice 2 rolls the info-popover onto the **3 hero-stat tiles that still lack one** (#activeLoansStat, #membersStat, #totalContributedStat), reusing **this page's OWN cycle-94 mechanism** — NOT admin's. No CSS port: `.hero-stat-popover` and its reveal rules already exist on this page.

**Mechanism (settled, cycle-102 map — not re-scouted):** `initHeroStatPopover(cardId, popoverId, opts={ignoreSelector})` at user_dashboard_sql.js:2050–2076 (toggles `.popover-open` on the card, maintains `aria-expanded`, click + keydown Enter/Space/Escape) plus a module-scope outside-click closer at 2080–2087. Already wired to 3 of 6 tiles at 1777–1783. The page's CSS reveals `.hero-stat-popover` on `.hero-stat:hover`, `.hero-stat:focus-within`, and `.hero-stat.popover-open` (user_dashboard.html:691–693); `.hero-stat-popover:empty` is hidden (697), so each new popover must contain literal text.

**DESIGN DECISION recorded (refines the cycle-102 "call initHeroStatPopover for all 3, pass ignoreSelector" note — ambiguous ≠ blocked):** `initHeroStatPopover`'s keydown handler (2067–2075) **unconditionally `preventDefault()`s Enter/Space** and `ignoreSelector` only guards the *click* handler, not keydown. So wiring it onto the two **natively-interactive** tiles — #membersStat (an `<a href>`) and #totalContributedStat (a `<button onclick>`) — would **hijack their built-in keyboard activation** (follow-link / open-modal), an accessibility regression `ignoreSelector` cannot prevent. Therefore:
- **#activeLoansStat** (a `<div role=button>` — no native Enter activation to break, and it already has an inner `#activeLoansBadge` mirroring #nextPaymentStat's badge): **DO wire `initHeroStatPopover` with `ignoreSelector: "#activeLoansBadge"`.** Pointer clicks still navigate via the existing inline `onclick`; the added keyboard toggle is a pure gain.
- **#membersStat (`<a>`) and #totalContributedStat (`<button>`):** **DO NOT wire `initHeroStatPopover`** — add the content-filled `.hero-stat-popover` div + `aria-describedby` + `position: relative` and let the page's existing `:hover`/`:focus-within` CSS reveal it (focusing the natively-focusable `<a>`/`<button>` matches `:focus-within`). Their tap/Enter keeps doing the action (navigate / open modal); the popover is a pointer/keyboard-focus enhancement. This preserves each tile's primary action with zero regression. (A full touch-toggle on these two would require converting them from `<a>`/`<button>` to a `role=button` div with an inner action element — a markup restructure out of scope for a tooltip rollout.)

**Microcopy is scope-accurate to this page's OWN shipped field definitions (not invented):** #totalContributed is set by `applyDashboardMonthFilter()` (user_dashboard_sql.js:582–599) as the member's OWN payments with `approvalStatus` in {`approved`,`completed`}, scoped to the selected month **or** the whole year (label shows `(Jul)` or `(2026)`) — so the copy must stay accurate for **either** scope (no hardcoded "this month").

## CYCLE 101 (2026-07-24) — group I, I5: CARD INFO "i" TOOLTIP ROLLOUT — **SCOUT DISPATCHED, cycle stops at scout**
Traces to GROUP I / I5 ("make the helper work and add it to many cards across the dashboards/analytics"). Sequencing per cycle-98's plan: I1→I2→I3 done, **I5 now**, then I4. The tooltip **mechanism already exists and works** (cycle-98 scout, SYSTEM_MAP Q3): `setupStatCardPopovers()` (admin_dashboard_sql.js:1343–1370) drives 4 `.stat-card-info-toggle` "i" buttons on admin_dashboard.html (1629/1637/1645/1653), each paired with a `.stat-card-popover` div (role="status") — click-toggle for touch, CSS `:hover`/`:focus-within` for desktop, keyboard/aria-expanded accessible. I5 = roll this existing pattern out to the cards that currently LACK it; it is NOT built from scratch.

**Card inventory that LACKS an "i" tooltip (from the maps, not re-derived):**
- **analytics.html** — NONE of its cards have tooltips: the four `.page-stat-value` tiles (`#totalIncome`/`#totalExpenses`/`#netProfit`/`#loanInterest`, html 154–171); the `#chartContainer` pie/breakdown cards (Contributions Collected / Loans Disbursed / Outstanding Arrears / Outstanding Loan Balances, dynamically-created `.breakdown-card` via `statCard()` analytics_sql.js:424–434); and the **new I3 `#accountingFiguresBlock`** ten accounting cards (also `.breakdown-card` via `statCard()`).
- **user_dashboard.html** — its stat tiles (Total Contributed / Pending Payments / Total Arrears / Active Loans / Group Members / Next Payment) have NO tooltips (cycle-98 audit found the "i" mechanism only on admin_dashboard).
- **admin_dashboard.html** — its four main stat tiles (Collections / Loans / Pending / Arrears) ALREADY have tooltips; no gap there. (Possible secondary cards unmapped — scout to confirm none are missed, but the four primary tiles are done.)

**Why a SCOUT first (not a direct exact-line brief):** rolling the mechanism cross-page hits three genuinely-UNMAPPED dependencies that would force DOM/CSS guesses into a brief (the failure mode the rules forbid):
1. **CSS location + cross-page reachability** — the `.stat-card-info-toggle` / `.stat-card-popover` / `.open` styles that make the mechanism visible are unmapped (inline in admin_dashboard.html vs. a shared `styles/*.css`). If inline, rollout must port them; if shared+linked, it's free. Determines whether I5 is a 1-file-per-page add or also needs CSS work.
2. **Mechanism sharing** — `setupStatCardPopovers()` lives INSIDE admin_dashboard_sql.js and is not (per the maps) an exported/shared module, so analytics_sql.js / user_dashboard_sql.js cannot call it as-is. Scout must confirm whether it's shareable (export/import) or must be copied per page, and whether it hard-depends on the `.stat-card` wrapper class (analytics cards are `.breakdown-card`, a different structure).
3. **Exact target-card DOM per page** — the internal DOM built by `statCard()` (analytics_sql.js:424–434, where an "i" button + popover would be injected) and the static stat-tile markup on user_dashboard.html + analytics' `.page-stat` tiles are not mapped to the line/element level needed to say "insert button X after node Y."

These are a bounded, read-only set of questions — a minimal targeted scout, not a survey. Cycle stops at the scout per the exact-line rule.

**Design decisions recorded (ambiguous ≠ blocked; hold for the post-scout frontend brief):**
- **Phased rollout, one page per frontend slice.** First I5 build slice will target the single best-mapped, most self-contained page the scout confirms (likely analytics.html, since its cards and the `statCard()` helper are already mapped and the new I3 block is the freshest owner-visible surface), reusing the mechanism per the scout's sharing verdict. user_dashboard is a following slice. This keeps each slice exact-line and low-risk rather than a three-page big-bang.
- **Content copy is per-card explanatory microcopy** (what the figure means / how it's derived), plain text set via `textContent` into the `.stat-card-popover` — never `innerHTML`, never user/server strings. Exact wording will be drafted in the frontend brief from each card's known meaning (the accounting-figure definitions are already recorded in CYCLE 97).
- **No behaviour/money change** — I5 is display/affordance only; it adds no API call, no arithmetic, touches no server figure.

Original backend brief below.

## CYCLE 97 (2026-07-23) — OWNER-DIRECTED group H, H5: SERVER-COMPUTED group ACCOUNTING SUMMARY (backend slice) — BRIEF DISPATCHED
Traces to group H (richer info + accounting) / B1 analytics. Backend-first: add a read-only, admin-gated `payments.accountingSummary` GET → `group_accounting_summary()` in payments.php that returns the group's whole financial position in minor units. This is genuinely ADDITIVE over `financial_reports_sql.js`, which today (a) sums income/disbursements/net CLIENT-side in floats, (b) reads interest from `loansSummary.totalInterest` which is `'0.00'` on every loan (real interest lives in `loan_payments.interestPortion` once repayments are approved), and (c) shows NO penalties-collected and NO cash-position line. UI wiring deferred to a later cycle (backend-first). No schema change (read-only aggregation).

**Money-rule ASSUMPTIONS recorded (defensible, clearly labelled):**
- `penaltiesOutstanding` = ledger definition = charged − collected − waived from `penalty_settlements` (clamp ≥ 0), labelled "recorded penalties outstanding" — distinct from the live-growing arrears penalties that `group_arrears_summary` re-derives on read. Not merged.
- `cashPosition` = totalContributed + loanRepaymentsReceived + contributionPenaltiesCollected − totalDisbursed. To avoid DOUBLE-COUNTING loan penalties (already inside `loan_payments.amount`), cashPosition uses only CONTRIBUTION penalties collected (`penalty_settlements.amountPaid WHERE paymentId IS NOT NULL`); the full `penaltiesCollected` is still reported as an informational line.
- Live sum-verification against the real DB is DEFERRED (DB throttled this session) — QA is logic/diff review only.

Convention in this file (see payment_settled_minor ~line 485–532): fetch the DECIMAL money column rows and sum ROW-WISE via money_to_minor() — do NOT use SQL SUM() on money columns, never floats. Emit each total with money_from_minor() (string). Helpers: money_to_minor(string):int (money.php:21), money_from_minor(int):string.

AND paymentId IS NOT NULL.

## CYCLE 94 (2026-07-23) — OWNER DIRECTIVE: "all class='page-stats' and cards to do something in user and admin dashboard. Not just decoration. They should even be informative on hover. Think banking and critical accounting." — DONE, QA PASS (lint + review, first pass)

Dispatched directly on the owner's explicit ask (not pulled from the U-SHELL/H-group queue below — those stay untouched/in-progress). Investigation first: catalogued every stat/card on `admin_dashboard.html` + `user_dashboard.html` and confirmed everything needed was buildable from ALREADY-fetched data — zero new API calls, no COMPLEXITY: high backend work required.

**`pages/admin_dashboard.html` + `scripts/admin_dashboard_sql.js`** — 4 stat cards (`#totalCollections`, `#activeLoans`, `#pendingApprovals`, `#totalArrears`) were clickable (`navigateToStatPage(...)`, preserved unchanged) but had zero hover/tap detail despite each already fetching richer breakdown data and discarding it. Added a `.stat-card-info-toggle` + `.stat-card-popover` per card, click/hover/keyboard-accessible (CSS `:hover`/`:focus-within` for desktop/keyboard, JS click-toggle with outside-click-close for touch — same dual-path principle as cycle 82's calendar day-details panel): Collections breaks down by `paymentType`; Active Loans shows total outstanding balance + active count (no invented "overdue" figure — confirmed not honestly derivable from already-fetched loan rows, correctly omitted rather than faked); Pending splits the combined count into separate payment/loan-request counts (both already computed pre-sum) with a link to `manage_loans.html`'s existing pending-requests tab; Arrears surfaces `arrears`/`penaltyAccrued`/`memberCount` from `groupData.groupArrears` (fetched every load, previously only `totalArrears` was ever shown). Also found and fixed, matching "not just decoration": the Collection Trends chart's range `<select>` (Last 6 months/Last year/All time) had ZERO JS reference anywhere — wired it to actually filter the chart's data by date client-side from already-fetched rows.

**`pages/user_dashboard.html` + `scripts/user_dashboard_sql.js`** — the plain "Pending" and "Arrears" hero-stats had NO interactivity at all (confirmed: no click, no hover). Added the same popover pattern: Pending lists up to 5 individual pending payments (type/date/amount) with a "+N more" cap; Arrears splits into "Arrears" + "Penalties" (previously only the combined total was ever shown, despite both fetched separately in `payments.obligations`); Next Payment got a popover showing obligation type + days-until-due/overdue (info not already on the card face). Correctly preserved two pre-existing behaviors the popover had to coexist with: `#totalArrears`'s (the inner value span) existing click-to-open-modal listener, and `#nextPaymentBadge`'s existing dismiss button — both scoped out of the new popover-toggle via an `ignoreSelector` so neither conflicts. `#activeLoansStat`/`#membersStat`/`#totalContributedStat` (already correctly clickable) and the entire Payment Calendar (built + QA-passed last cycle) were explicitly left untouched.

**ui-designer polish pass** gave both sets of popovers real, visually-consistent treatment (same shadow/radius/padding tokens on both pages, reusing this app's existing floating-panel convention rather than inventing a new one) and caught a real bug: `.stats-grid`/`.hero-stats`'s `overflow-x: auto` at the ≤768px breakpoint was implicitly computing `overflow-y: auto` too (CSS overflow-pairing), silently clipping every popover below the fold on mobile — fixed with an explicit `overflow: visible` override. Also added severity color-coding (danger/success, reusing the calendar's existing tokens) to overdue/on-time text in the popovers.

QA: LINT PASS — syntax clean across all 4 files, no `innerHTML` anywhere in the new popover code, every referenced id exists, `formatCurrency`/`formatCurrencyFromMinor` reused correctly for minor-unit sums vs. server strings. REVIEW PASS — confirmed all 4 admin `navigateToStatPage` click-throughs and both user-dashboard pre-existing behaviors (`#totalArrears` modal, `#nextPaymentBadge` dismiss) are unsuppressed by the new toggle listeners (toggle handler correctly checks `ignoreSelector` before acting, and calls `stopPropagation` where needed); confirmed the Collections breakdown's grouping key matches the real `paymentType` field used elsewhere in the same file (not a guessed field); confirmed Arrears popover numbers come straight from the already-fetched object with zero re-derivation (can't drift from the number on the card face); confirmed zero new API calls added and no `api/` file touched; confirmed keyboard reachability (real `<button>`s on admin, `tabindex="0"`+keydown on user-dashboard, `:focus-within` CSS on both) satisfies "informative on hover... on all devices," not hover-only. One non-blocking a11y nit noted (hero-stat cards could additionally carry `role="button"` alongside `aria-expanded` — cosmetic, keyboard access already works) — not filed as a fix, left as a future minor polish note.

**Not yet manually tested in a live browser** (static + QA verification only) — recommend the owner check the popovers on both dashboards, especially at a mobile width, to confirm the overflow fix reads well in practice.

## CYCLE 92 (2026-07-23) — OWNER-DIRECTED group H (richer info + accounting), H2: member ACCOUNT STATEMENT / LEDGER — SCOUT DISPATCHED, cycle stops at scout
**Scope tracing (H is NOT a new checklist box):** the A–F PROJECT COMPLETE WHEN checklist is fully `[x]` (cycle 47). Member views own; admin views any member via the same endpoint's admin-only `?uid` override (the pattern `payments.php my_obligations` and `loans.eligibility` already use). Plus CSV export reusing `exports.php` (`export_csv_cell()` guard, `require_role(EXPORT_ADMIN_ROLES)`).

Provisional home: `financial_reports.html` (admin, any member via a member picker feeding `?uid`) for the admin path, and a read-only "My statement" section on `user_analytics.html` (member self, no uid) — both consume the ONE endpoint. Finalised when the UI objective is dispatched (after the backend endpoint lands).

## CYCLE 82 (2026-07-23) — OWNER DIRECTIVE: "Payment calendar should have most details properly showing on hover or on screen on all devices" — DONE, QA PASS (lint + review, first pass)

Cycle 81's new calendar only exposed event details via `dot.title = ev.label` — a native browser tooltip, which doesn't work AT ALL on touch devices (no hover) and is unstyled/inconsistent even on desktop. Replaced with a click/tap-to-reveal on-screen details panel that works identically on every device — the standard "select a day, see its agenda" calendar pattern, no hover dependency.

**Built (`pages/user_dashboard.html` + `scripts/user_dashboard_sql.js` only):** `.calendar-day` cells that actually have events become `tabindex="0" role="button"` with a real `aria-label` (day + event count) and click+keydown (Enter/Space) handlers — cells with zero events stay exactly as before, not artificially interactive. Activating a cell marks it `.selected` (single-selection — clears any previous one first) and renders that day's events into a new `#calendarDayDetails` panel: date heading + one row per event showing dot/label/derived status (Overdue/Paid/Upcoming, read from the SAME `overdue`/`paid` booleans that already color the dot — can't drift out of sync with it). Navigating months (`shiftCalendarMonth`) clears the panel + selection state BEFORE re-rendering, so a stale day's details never linger and a same-day-number cell in the new month is never falsely shown as pre-selected. The `title` tooltip was kept too (harmless, still a nice-to-have for desktop mouse users) — added on top of the panel, not instead of it.

**ui-designer polish pass caught and fixed a real bug:** `.selected` had originally duplicated `.today`'s gradient fill, making a selected-but-not-today day visually indistinguishable from today. Fixed to an inset accent ring only, so the two states compose cleanly when a day is both. Also added: missing `:focus-visible` ring on the newly-tabbable day cells (this app requires visible focus states everywhere), Overdue/Paid/Upcoming status color-coding in the panel (reusing existing danger/success tokens, not new colors), a touch-target gap (a mobile breakpoint had `min-height: auto !important` silently canceling the 44px floor once cells became genuinely interactive), and mobile row-wrap fixes for the detail rows.

QA: LINT PASS — syntax clean, no `innerHTML` anywhere in the new functions, keyboard guard matches the app's existing `e.target === el` idiom, panel-clear correctly sequenced before month re-render. REVIEW PASS — confirmed keyboard reachability (tabindex+role+keydown all present together, sane tab order), confirmed non-event days stay fully inert, confirmed single-selection correctness including harmless re-click-same-day, confirmed status can never contradict the dot's own color, confirmed `.selected`/`.today` compose correctly by reading the actual CSS (not assumed), confirmed `aria-label` is built from real day/count data, confirmed zero new API calls (pure re-presentation of already-fetched `calendarEventsMap`) and diff confined to the 2 named files.

**Not yet manually clicked through in a live browser** (static + QA verification only) — recommend the owner try tapping a day with events on both a phone/tablet and desktop mouse, plus keyboard-only Tab+Enter navigation, once deployed.

## CYCLE 81 (2026-07-23) — OWNER DIRECTIVE (user_dashboard): fix hero/main-content spacing, move Quick Actions into the hero, build the Payment Calendar — DONE, QA PASS (lint + review, first pass)

Three related asks on `pages/user_dashboard.html` + `scripts/user_dashboard_sql.js`, all dispatched together since they touch overlapping regions of the same two files.

1. **Hero-to-content spacing** — cycle 80's fix (removing a negative `margin-top` that caused the sticky-nav overlap) correctly killed the overlap but left the transition between `.hero-section` and `.main-content` flush/abrupt. Fixed: `.main-content` now has `margin: var(--bn-space-8) auto 0` (desktop), `margin-top: var(--bn-space-5)` at ≤768px — both POSITIVE, confirmed the old negative-margin bug is not reintroduced.

2. **Quick Actions relocated into the hero.** Moved verbatim (`#requestLoanBtn`, `#uploadPaymentBtn`, `#upcomingPaymentsBtn`, `#viewPaymentDetailsBtn`, `#viewMembersBtn`, `#viewRulesBtn`, `#viewGroupsBtn`, Analytics/Settings links) from a `.content-grid`/`.content-card` into a new `.hero-quick-actions` block inside `<section class="hero-section">`, right after `.hero-stats`. Every id confirmed to appear exactly once post-move; the old wrapper was removed cleanly (no hollow leftover card); the Notifications card that used to share `.content-grid` with Quick Actions now stands alone correctly. New scoped CSS (`.hero-section .quick-actions .action-btn` + states) for the dark-background context, base `.action-btn`/`.quick-actions` used elsewhere untouched.

3. **Payment Calendar — real bug found: this was dead markup with ZERO logic.** `#paymentCalendar`, the month-nav buttons, and the full legend/CSS all already existed in the page, but `grep`-confirmed nothing in `user_dashboard_sql.js` ever touched any of it — the "calendar" had never actually been built, just scaffolded. Built from scratch this cycle: `fetchActiveLoanSchedules` (one best-effort `apiGet("loans.get", {loanId})` per the caller's OWN approved/disbursed loans only — individually try/caught so one failed fetch can't break the whole calendar), `buildCalendarEvents` (merges `payments.obligations` seed/monthly/service-fee data + loan schedule rows into a day-keyed event map, reusing the file's existing `parseServerDate`/`toMinor`/`pickOwedString` helpers — no new date-parsing logic), `renderCalendarGrid` (real month grid via `createElement`/`textContent`, using the pre-existing `.calendar-day`/`.calendar-event-note.<type>` class contract), `shiftCalendarMonth` (re-renders from the cached event map, no re-fetch per month click). Wired to `#calendarPrevMonth`/`#calendarNextMonth`, called from `loadDashboard()` alongside the other render calls, defaults to the real current month.

**ui-designer polish pass:** confirmed hero contrast/touch-targets/focus-visible were already correct (reused existing tokens properly, nothing to fix); found and fixed one real accessibility gap — the calendar's "Paid" dot relied on opacity alone to read as distinct from an unpaid dot of the same color (a color/brightness-only cue) — added an inset ring (`box-shadow: inset 0 0 0 1.5px rgba(255,255,255,0.9)`) to both the rendered dot and the legend swatch so they match and the state reads by shape, not just opacity.

QA: LINT PASS — syntax clean, all button ids match exactly once, calendar code confirmed `createElement`/`textContent` only (no `innerHTML`), `formatCurrency` reused for every amount. REVIEW PASS — confirmed no negative-margin regression, confirmed `fetchActiveLoanSchedules` scoped to caller's own active loans only (not all group loans) with true best-effort per-loan error handling (not an all-or-nothing `Promise.all`), confirmed overdue/paid classification logic is correct (a paid-off past obligation is NOT flagged overdue), confirmed `.today`/`.other-month` day-grid math and month/year rollover are correct with no click-handlers on non-real days, confirmed legend/dot parity on the accessibility fix, confirmed no `api/` file touched and diff confined to the 2 named files. Per-member loan count is capped by `group_rules.loanRulesMaxActiveLoansByMember` (a bounded int), so the one-fetch-per-active-loan pattern is not an unbounded N+1 risk.

**Not yet manually clicked through in a live browser** (static + QA verification only) — recommend the owner try month navigation, hover states, and the calendar at mobile widths (640px/375px) once deployed.

## CYCLE 80 (2026-07-22) — OWNER-REPORTED BUG: "main-content getting hidden behind top-nav" on user_dashboard + admin_dashboard — PARTIALLY DONE, QA PASS on the confirmed half

**`pages/user_dashboard.html` — CONFIRMED root cause, FIXED, QA PASS.** `.main-content` (inline `<style>`, was line 593-600) had `margin-top: calc(-1 * var(--bn-space-8))` (-32px) plus a `z-index: 2` that could never win anyway (`.top-nav` — a SIBLING element injected by `nav_sql.js:486-488` as `document.body`'s first child, directly before `#mainContent` — uses `z-index: var(--z-fixed)` = 300, vs. design-system.css's z-index scale where 2 loses to everything). The negative margin pulled `.main-content` up into `.top-nav`'s sticky space, so the top ~32px of the dashboard's first content card rendered visibly underneath the nav bar on every load — reproducible, not viewport/timing-dependent. Fix: deleted both the `margin-top` and `z-index: 2` lines, kept `position: relative` (still needed — `#nextPaymentBadge`/`#activeLoansBadge` are absolutely-positioned relative to it). QA PASS: CSS well-formed, confirmed no other rule depended on the deleted lines.

**`pages/admin_dashboard.html` — NOT REPRODUCED statically, NOT touched.** Exhaustive check found no equivalent defect: `.main-content` (inline, ~line 237-240) has only `margin-left`/`min-height`, zero negative margins anywhere in the file; `.topbar` (its nav bar) is structurally different from user_dashboard's `.top-nav` — it's injected as a CHILD *inside* `#mainContent` (`nav_sql.js:744-745`), not a sibling before it, so the sibling-overlap mechanism that broke user_dashboard doesn't apply here at all; `.sidebar` is `position:fixed` but only 280px wide, doesn't reach into the topbar's title area; no dynamic JS style mutation (`marginTop`/`.style.top`) found in `admin_dashboard_sql.js` or `nav_sql.js`. **Also separately confirmed as a non-cause (initially suspected):** neither `user_dashboard.html` nor ANY of the 13 admin pages ever gets a `class="admin-page"`/`class="user-page"` on `<body>` (only 5 of 6 user pages do) — but since each page's `nav_sql.js` variant only ever injects ONE nav element (never both `.top-nav` and `.topbar` on the same page), the CSS toggle rules gated on that body class are inconsequential either way; this is pre-existing and not the cause of either the confirmed or the unconfirmed report.

**Awaiting owner input on admin_dashboard:** either the owner was describing the same symptom loosely and it's actually only `user_dashboard.html` (now fixed) — or it reproduces only under a specific condition static analysis can't see (a particular viewport/breakpoint, a specific browser, or only after some dynamic content load). **Needs: the owner's screen size/browser, or a screenshot, before a second investigation pass is spent on `admin_dashboard.html`.** Not marked BLOCKED (no product decision needed, just more repro detail) — parked here as the next thing to act on once that detail arrives.

## CYCLE 76 (2026-07-22) — OWNER-REPORTED BUG: SPA modal/overlay markup lost on navigation — DONE, QA PASS (lint + review, first pass)
Owner reported two things: (1) the sidebar reloading when `.dashboard-content` changes, and wanted only the topbar title to update on navigation — investigated and confirmed this is ALREADY correctly built (cycle 56/66 SPA work): `spa-router.js` never touches the sidebar/topbar chrome, only swaps `.dashboard-content`, and `nav_sql.js`'s `updateActiveNav()` updates the active nav item + topbar title. The in-content loader (`showLoader`/`hideLoader`) is already scoped strictly to `.dashboard-content`, never rendered elsewhere. **No code change needed for this part — architecture already matches the ask.** (2) Owner reported: on `manage_loans.html`, clicking "New Loan" does nothing.

**Root cause (new finding, distinct from cycle 70's undefined-onclick bug class):** every admin page places its modal overlays (and its own `#spinner` loading overlay) as **direct children of `<body>`, after `</main>`** — i.e. outside `.dashboard-content` and outside `#mainContent` entirely. `spa-router.js`'s `navigateTo()` only ever swaps `.dashboard-content`; it never brought this body-level markup along on an SPA navigation. So arriving at `manage_loans.html` via the sidebar (not a hard page load) meant `#newLoanModal` never existed in the live DOM, and `manage_loans_sql.js`'s `openNewLoanModal()` → `showModal("newLoanModal")` → `getElementById` returned `null` → silent no-op. Confirmed the identical body-level-modal pattern also on `manage_payments.html`, `manage_members.html`, `interest_penalties.html`, and `admin_dashboard.html` (`#statModalOverlay`) — so this was an app-wide bug affecting "New Loan" and likely several other modal-opening buttons app-wide whenever reached via SPA nav rather than a hard load/refresh, not limited to the one button the owner named.

**Fix (single file, `scripts/spa-router.js` only):** generalized the file's existing `LOCAL_STYLE_ATTR`/`tagExistingLocalStyles()`/`swapLocalStyles()` tag-and-replace pattern (previously only for `<style>` tags) to a new parallel mechanism — `LOCAL_BODY_ATTR`, `SHARED_BODY_CHROME_SELECTOR` (excludes `#mainContent`, `#sidebar`, `#sidebarOverlay`, `.mobile-nav`, `.top-nav`, `#mobileMenuOverlay`, `#mobileMenu`, `#toastContainer` — verified against `nav_sql.js`'s actual injected chrome), `isSyncablePageBodyElement()`, `tagExistingLocalBodyElements()`, `swapLocalBodyElements(targetDoc)` — called once per navigation right after the existing `swapLocalStyles(doc)` call. Tags/removes previously-synced page-owned body elements each nav, deep-clones (`cloneNode(true)`) the target page's equivalent elements in fresh. Explicitly excludes `<script>` tags (no duplicate-eval risk) and `#mainContent` (never touched — that region stays the separate `.dashboard-content` swap).

QA: LINT PASS (haiku) — valid syntax, naming/JSDoc consistent with file, no stray debug output, script-tag exclusion confirmed, `#mainContent` exclusion confirmed. REVIEW PASS (sonnet) — all 7 acceptance criteria verified against the diff + cross-checked against `nav_sql.js`'s real chrome selectors and `manage_loans.html`'s markup: no double-nav-accumulation on repeat A→B→A visits (tag-then-remove-then-clone runs identically every time), deep clone confirmed (nested form controls preserved), no new `innerHTML`/XSS surface (only `cloneNode`/`setAttribute`/`appendChild`/`remove`), shared chrome never reordered/touched. No scope creep — diff confined to the one file.

**Not yet manually smoke-tested in a live browser this cycle** (static+QA verification only, per this session's tooling) — recommend the owner click through New Loan (and a couple of the other now-fixed pages' modal buttons) once after this deploys, to close the loop with real-browser confirmation.

## CYCLE 65 (2026-07-21) — ITEM 1 ROOT CAUSE CONFIRMED + FIX. Live investigation (curl against production API + read of `scripts/manage_loans_sql.js` / `pages/manage_loans.html`) resolved cycle-64 item 1 to root cause **(a) filter/information-architecture defect** — NOT a click bug, NOT a data-state issue. The "Active Loans" tab is CONFIRMED CORRECT against live data (LN-0001, status `approved`, genuinely appears). The real defect: two of the 7 tab labels ("Approved (Awaiting Disbursement)", "Disbursed Loans") describe a disbursement distinction that **cannot exist on this system** — `loans.approve` prices, schedules, and effectively disburses in one atomic call, so no loan ever sits "awaiting disbursement" and no endpoint ever sets status `disbursed`. A user clicks those reasonable-sounding tabs, sees nothing, and concludes "the filter is broken," when the correct tab (Active) sits right beside them and works. Traces to the **C5/C6** ported-page surface; no checklist box reopens; cycles not renumbered.

### Verified facts (this session — live API + grep-confirmed, not assumed)
- **Reachable loan statuses only** (confirmed by the code that SETs each): `pending` (loans.request), `approved` (loans.php:442 — this loan is now active/disbursed in practice), `rejected` (loans.php:532), `completed` (repayments.php:560, set when remainingBalance hits 0). **`disbursed` is a dead ENUM value — no endpoint ever assigns it.** **`defaulted` is ALSO dead** — grep of `api/handlers/loans.php` + `repayments.php` shows NO `UPDATE loans SET status='defaulted'`; it appears only in `exports.php:39` as a member of an export inclusion *list*, never assigned. So the system produces exactly 4 statuses.
- **`loans.list` (list_loans, loans.php:156-162) returns per loan:** `status, repaymentPeriod, approvedAt, remainingBalance, completedAt` (among others) — but **NO `dueDate` / schedule at the list level.** Per-installment `dueDate` is only returned by `loans.get` (loans.php:223-232) and `repayments.balance`, one loan at a time. So a per-installment "overdue" cannot be computed in `renderLoans()` from data the page already holds — BUT a coarse maturity-based overdue CAN: `approvedAt + repaymentPeriod months < now` AND `remainingBalance > 0` uses only fields already in the list rows. This needs NO backend change and NO extra fetch.
- Current switch (`scripts/manage_loans_sql.js:485-515`): `pending`→status==='pending' (works); `approved`→hardcoded `[]` (dead); `disbursed`→status==='disbursed' (never reachable, always empty); `active`→status IN(approved,disbursed) (CONFIRMED WORKING); `repaid`→status==='completed'; `cancelled`→status==='rejected'; `overdue`→hardcoded `[]` (dead). Existing helper `numberOf(loan.remainingBalance)` (used :461/:550/:817) already reads balance as a number for threshold checks.

### Owner decision (verbatim)
Offered (a) remove the 2 dead tabs or (b) gray them out, owner chose NEITHER: **"These should show what they are meant to and able to filter."** → REDEFINE the tabs so every one maps to a status/slice this system can actually produce.

### RECOMMENDED TAB SET (7 → 5; every tab reachable, labels non-overlapping)
| Label | data-tab | Filter predicate | Reachable via |
|---|---|---|---|
| Pending Requests | `pending` | `status === 'pending'` | loans.request |
| Active Loans | `active` | `status === 'approved' \|\| status === 'disbursed'` (keep OR — harmless if `disbursed` ever wired later; keeps parity with server `summary.activePrincipal` which counts both) | loans.approve |
| Overdue | `overdue` | `(status==='approved' \|\| status==='disbursed')` **AND** `numberOf(remainingBalance) > 0` **AND** maturity `approvedAt + repaymentPeriod months` is before now | computed client-side from list fields |
| Repaid | `repaid` | `status === 'completed'` | repayments.approve (balance→0) |
| Cancelled | `cancelled` | `status === 'rejected'` | loans.reject |
**REMOVED:** "Approved (Awaiting Disbursement)" (`data-tab="approved"`, case always `[]`) and "Disbursed Loans" (`data-tab="disbursed"`, status never set) — both describe a disbursement step this system does not have; their meaning is fully covered by Active. Removing them (rather than leaving two always-empty tabs) IS the "show what they are meant to and able to filter" outcome, since a tab that can never show a row is not filtering anything it is "able to."
**REDEFINED:** "Overdue" moves from a permanent `[]` stub to a real, reachable slice computed from data the page already fetches — active loans past their repayment period still carrying a balance. Overdue is a labelled *subset* of Active (superset/subset overlap is intentional and standard — Active = the full working set matching the server summary; Overdue = the attention subset). Note if owner later wants Active to EXCLUDE overdue rows, that is a one-line change — flag, don't assume.
**Reasoning this beats the alternatives:** deleting/graying the 2 dead tabs was explicitly rejected by the owner; leaving 3 tabs (approved/disbursed/active) all pointing at the identical filter would be visually redundant, not a fix. Reducing to 5 correctly-labelled tabs — each mapping to one of the 4 real statuses plus one genuinely-computable Overdue slice — makes every tab "show what it is meant to and able to filter" with zero backend work and no money math.

## CYCLE 66 (2026-07-21) — THREE OWNER-REPORTED ISSUES, all RE-SURFACING cycle 57 (which "closed" these exact three but shipped fixes that did not hold in practice). (1) PRIORITY: UI is "flakey" — CSS does not load correctly until a manual page reload. (2) `settings.html` shows the wrong email below the avatar. (3) `settings.html` other tab buttons (e.g. Security) do not respond to clicks. All three trace to the **C6** SPA/ported-page surface (owner-directed correctness on an already-`[x]` checklist — same class as cycles 55-65); no PROJECT COMPLETE WHEN box reopens; cycles not renumbered. **ONE objective dispatched this cycle: Issue 1** (app-wide, actively breaking, root-caused below). Issues 2 & 3 share one file (`settings_sql.js`) and a different root cause from Issue 1 → queued as the next cycle, NOT bundled here.

### Root cause of Issue 1 — CONFIRMED from the shipped router code this session (not assumed):
`scripts/spa-router.js`'s `navigateTo()` DOES sync `<head>` CSS (cycle 57 built it): `syncStylesheetLinks()` (:273-296) additively appends any missing `<link rel="stylesheet">` to the live `<head>`; `swapLocalStyles()` (:310-331) replaces the outgoing page's inline `<style>` block(s). **But the appended `<link>` loads ASYNCHRONOUSLY and there is NO onload gate** — `syncStylesheetLinks()` appends the link (:291-294) and `navigateTo()` immediately reveals the swapped content at `currentContent.replaceWith(newContent)` (:385). So on the FIRST SPA navigation to a page whose stylesheet is not yet in `<head>` (e.g. `settings.html`, whose `<link>` set differs from the other admin pages per cycle 57's md5 finding), the new content paints against not-yet-loaded CSS → the "flakey until reload" flash. A hard reload works because a full page load render-blocks on ALL its `<head>` stylesheets before first paint. This is EXACTLY the assumption cycle 57 got wrong — BUILD_PLAN:217 recorded "`<link>` insertion is non-blocking by nature; no special await needed"; that unawaited async is the residual defect. Inline `<style>` swap (`swapLocalStyles`) is synchronous (textContent) and applies instantly — NOT the problem; only external `<link>` is.

### The fix (localized to spa-router.js): gate the content reveal on the NEWLY-APPENDED stylesheets' load
`syncStylesheetLinks()` must return the set of `<link>` elements it newly appended (only the genuinely-new ones — never wait on stylesheets already present, so repeat navigation stays instant). `navigateTo()` must, after the CSS sync and BEFORE `currentContent.replaceWith(newContent)` (:385), await those new links' `load`/`error` events — with a bounded per-navigation timeout fallback (e.g. ~1500-2000ms) so a slow or failing stylesheet can NEVER hang navigation. Zero new links (the common repeat-nav case) → the await resolves immediately, no added latency. This makes a swapped-in page render styled-on-arrival on the very first visit, no reload.

**`pages/manage_payments.html` — DONE, QA PASS.** `ui-designer` polish pass, CSS/accessibility only, `design-system.css` tokens (existing navy palette, NOT the generic Japandi theme — this app already has its own design system, correctly not overwritten). Changes: `aria-label` on all icon-only modal-close/clear buttons; removed dead inline `cursor:pointer` (already in `.clickable-stat`); `:focus-visible` states added to `.quick-action-card`/`.payment-tab`/`.clickable-stat` (none had any before); `.payment-tab:hover:not(.active)` state added; `min-height:44px` on `.payment-tab` for touch-target compliance; local breakpoint unified from ad-hoc `640px` to the project-standard `768px`, plus a `480px` rule for the quick-action grid. QA independently verified: diff confined to this file, no JS-load-bearing id/class/data-attribute touched (cross-checked against `manage_payments_sql.js`'s selectors), no hardcoded color/spacing outside `--bn-*` tokens, no breakpoint conflict, HTML well-formed, no click-handler/button-text change. **DONE.**

**Flagged, not fixed (needs a behavior change, not CSS):** the 4 `.page-stat.clickable-stat` divs and `.quick-action-card` divs are click-driven via JS but are plain `<div>`s — not keyboard-reachable (no `tabindex`/`role="button"`/keydown handler). ui-designer correctly declined to add `tabindex` without matching JS (would create a focusable-but-non-functional control). Needs a `frontend-specialist` follow-up to add real `<button>` semantics or a keydown handler — noted, not yet dispatched.

**TEMPLATE PATTERNS established this cycle, to reuse verbatim on every subsequent page in the rollout:**
1. `:focus-visible { outline: 2px solid var(--bn-accent); outline-offset: 2px; }` on any custom interactive class not already covered by shared `.btn`/`.sidebar-nav-item` focus rules.
2. Local per-page breakpoints snap to the project standard set (480/768/1024/1280), never arbitrary values.
3. Custom pill/tab controls get explicit `min-height: 44px` alongside padding — don't rely on padding+font-size alone for the 44×44 touch target.
4. Every icon-only button (×, modal-close, clear/remove) gets an `aria-label` — this repeats on nearly every page.
5. Delete single-property inline `style="..."` wherever the element's class already declares the same property (dead-weight cleanup, found repeatedly).

**OWNER DECISION: full app rollout, admin pages first then user pages, in the order listed above.** Proceeding page-by-page (2 concurrent max per the max-concurrent-specialist rail), each with a QA pass before moving on, applying the 5 template patterns verbatim.

Every OTHER list-heavy page instead uses a hand-rolled `createXCard()`-div pattern that renders identically (always stacked) at every screen size, desktop included — that's the actual gap.

Converted the pending-approvals list from always-div-cards (`createPaymentCard()`) to a real `<table class="table table-responsive">` (7 columns: Member, Type, Amount, Arrears, Status, Details, Actions), populated by a new `createPaymentRow()`. - **Retry (1, per policy):** one-line fix restoring `· ${formatDate(payment.paidAt || payment.createdAt)}` to the Amount cell.
- **ui-designer polish pass (parallel):** Details column wrap/max-width, `.loan-actions` CSS added (this class existed in JS with ZERO CSS anywhere in the repo — a real pre-existing gap, not introduced by this cycle), 44px touch target + focus-visible on the action buttons, mobile action-button-fill-width treatment.
- **QA pass 2 (re-verify both together): PASS.** Confirmed the date fix landed correctly and is the only retry change, confirmed the polish pass touched only local CSS (no JS-selected id/class disturbed), confirmed every field the old card showed is still present somewhere in the new table, confirmed `emptyState` (used elsewhere) wasn't broken by the specific-to-this-path `emptyTableRow` replacement, confirmed no approve/reject business logic touched. **PILOT DONE.**

### Candidate pages for rollout (surveyed via grep of every `createXCard`/`buildXCard`-style list-rendering function across `scripts/`)

**Poor fit — deliberately NOT recommended for table conversion (cards are the right pattern here):** `contacts.html`'s member-card (directory/contact-card with phone/email/whatsapp tap-to-call links — a table would be awkward for icon-link-heavy content, not simple text columns), `admin_dashboard.html`'s due-payment widget cards (small "due soon" list, not a full browsable dataset), `messages.html`/`invitation_code_sql.js`/`user_dashboard.html`'s own group-switcher cards (small counts, card is the natural UI for a chooser, not a data table).

## CYCLE 70 (2026-07-21) — OWNER DIRECTIVE: "several admin buttons still not working like taking loan payments, payments, a lot of buttons"
Live-testing was skipped in favor of a fast, high-confidence static sweep (same bug CLASS already found and fixed once this session — an `onclick="fn()"` attribute referencing a function that is either never defined, or defined only at module scope without a `window.` export, so it silently `ReferenceError`s in the browser and the button does nothing). Grep-confirmed across every admin/user page. Findings, in fix-priority order:

1. **`handleMobileNavLogout()` — undefined everywhere in the repo.** Referenced by static mobile-bottom-nav "Sign Out" buttons on 6 pages: `contacts.html:453`, `loan_payments.html:270`, `messages.html:358`, `user_analytics.html:364`, `user_dashboard.html:2148`, `view_rules.html:262`. Every mobile sign-out button is dead. Highest impact — repo-wide, not just admin.
2. **`handleSwitchToAdmin()` — correctly defined+exported as `window.handleSwitchToAdmin` in `user_dashboard_sql.js:376`, but referenced from 2 pages that never load that script:** `loan_payments.html:266`, `contacts.html:449` (both load their own `_sql.js`, not `user_dashboard_sql.js`). Dead "Switch to Admin" mobile-nav button on those 2 pages.
3. **`clearPOPUpload()` — undefined, `pages/manage_payments.html:514`.** Removing an uploaded proof-of-payment file does nothing.
4. **Adjacent bug, same feature:** the POP (proof-of-payment) file-preview is never wired up at all — no `change` listener anywhere populates `#recordPaymentPOPFileName` or un-hides `#recordPaymentPOPPreview` (grep-confirmed only one read-at-submit-time reference in `manage_payments_sql.js:709`, nothing else). Uploading a file gives zero visual confirmation.
5. **`closeMessageModal()` referenced by `onclick` at `contacts.html:464,481` but defined at MODULE scope in `contacts_sql.js` with no `window.` export** — identical mechanism to items 1-2. The modal's × and Cancel buttons are dead (backdrop-click close still works, wired internally via `addEventListener`, so the modal isn't fully stuck — just its two explicit close controls).
6. **Not a bug, park for a future polish pass:** `#applyPenaltyModal` + `#confirmApplyPenalty` on `manage_payments.html` have zero listeners, but this is INTENTIONAL per the file's own header comment — penalties are server-computed live, no "apply" action exists, `applyPenaltyBtn` correctly shows an info toast instead of opening this modal. The modal markup itself is orphaned dead HTML (never opened by anything) — recommend deleting it in a future cleanup, not urgent, not a functional bug.

**Checked clean, no findings:** `manage_payments.html`'s core payment flow (recordPaymentBtn, tabs, filters, modal submit — all wired), `loan_payments.html`'s core loan-payment flow (makePaymentBtn, payBtn, modal submit — all wired, only the 2 repo-wide nav bugs above affect this page), `manage_loans.html` (all quick actions wired), `admin_dashboard.html` (all onclick targets correctly `window.`-exported). Not yet individually swept for the "dead id + zero listener" pattern (only checked for undefined-onclick): `approve_registrations.html`, `analytics.html`, `financial_reports.html`, `seed_money_overview.html`, `contributions_overview.html`, `interest_penalties.html`, `broadcast_notifications.html`, `settings.html` — flag as a candidate follow-up sweep if the owner reports something more specific on those pages.
